# College program attendance sources — research answer (Codex)

Status: saved Oct 27, 2026 (Kylie: "here is other answer"), in full as delivered, with its companion fixture file `ncaa-attendance-fixtures-codex.json` (the same game rows as JSON, from the same Codex output folder). Not folded: it becomes a proposal. Junior-college football stays as it is until a proposal from this answer (Kylie, Oct 27: "agree with your leans").

**Research read dates: October 26–27, 2026 (America/Los_Angeles). Expanded research completed within accessible sources; remaining gaps are explicit.** Prepared for Fan/Friction build 7.1, M35. This is research for a proposal, not a build authorization; no app changes were made.

**Finding:** school and league sources do publish free game-by-game announced attendance for the sampled NCAA, NAIA, junior-college and Canadian programs. Completeness varies by game and sport, even within one vendor. A public page, a supported Attendance scoring field and a season average are three different findings. No verified free announced attendance series was found for the sampled Mexican university-football programs in the documents checked.

**Worked-data scope:** 1,114 distinct program-game rows are listed in full in section 5. The expanded baseball/softball audit has verified 448 of 448 exact game IDs issued by the eight selected season indexes: USC/Boston College baseball and Long Beach State/Boston College softball, 2025 and 2026. The primary sample spans 16 school/sport/gender combinations and 32 program-seasons. The other worked families include two seasons each of football, men’s and women’s basketball, men’s hockey, women’s volleyball and women’s soccer in two covered metros. Published completed exhibition results are separate rows where found. These are a substantial verification sample, not a census of every college program in every metro; many older seasons and some actual venues and start-time/timezone details remain unverified. No school/venue median is computed from incomplete metadata.

**Labels:** **announced** means an official school, league or NCAA publication; **reported** means a named outlet quotes a number; **estimated** means arithmetic performed here, with its formula stated. “Announced” identifies the publisher, not the counting method. A school’s underlying estimate can still be its officially announced crowd. No capacity-derived crowd, invented game, interpolated attendance or invented zero is used.

**Source dates:** links in the retained platform/national-source tables were read on October 26, 2026, except additions explicitly dated October 27; expanded coverage and fixture rows state their attendance-source read date. Supplementary metadata was read October 26–27. A document’s season, publication/as-of date and research read date are separate. Blank cells mean absent from the checked publication or not independently verified; they do not establish absence from every source. “Not published in this document,” “Not found,” and “Inaccessible/unverified” are kept distinct.

Public research reads respected access controls and published crawl rules. The expanded school-page audit used at least 31 seconds between requests to each host after their robots files specified a 30-second crawl interval. This one-off personal research is separate from any future recurring app collection or redistribution. SIDEARM’s terms expressly allow personal downloading/copying; WMT and Disney/ESPN impose explicit automated-access restrictions. See section 2 and the national-source table.

## 1. Coverage by metro, school, sport and gender

Access status is source-specific. Public/no-key describes the observed read, not an open-data license. SIDEARM §2.3 permits personal downloading/copying and requires written permission for copying/storage outside personal use. The sampled USC, Boston College and Long Beach State robots files allow sports paths subject to a 30-second interval; they disallow other paths, including `/documents/`. Those rules are not a blanket ingestion license. WMT’s terms restrict automated interaction without prior written consent. RSEQ/Presto reuse terms remain unresolved. Vendor applicability inferred from a route is explicitly labeled **inference**; school-specific terms were not silently borrowed from another school.

**Coverage boundary:** these are checked programs, not a complete roster. Platform-wide applicability beyond these rows is **inference**. The presence of one attendance line does not establish that every completed home game has a figure. An opponent-hosted box is useful evidence if home/away, actual venue and the same game are independently reconciled.

## 2. Platforms, public routes and permission status

| Platform / publication | Attendance on box / schedule | Machine-readable or export route; documentation; key | Terms for free nightly app read | Historical reach actually established | Examples, season and verification |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SIDEARM current / legacy | Yes in checked boxes: Game Information → Attendance. Season PDF results use Attend, Att. or Att. #. Some season HTML Results tables include it; schedules/other sport cume pages need not. | Public HTML `/sports/{sport}/stats/{season}/{opponent}/boxscore/{id}`; legacy `/boxscore.aspx?id={id}&path={sport}`. Public cume PDF `https://s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com/sidearm.nextgen.sites/{domain}/stats/{code}/{year}/pdf/cume.pdf`. Publication pattern verified for listed PDFs, **undocumented as an API**. No key for these public reads. No documented anonymous attendance JSON/XML API established. Embedded season attendance totals are not game-level records. | Personal downloading/copying expressly permitted by §2.3; copying/storage beyond personal use requires written permission. [SIDEARM terms, last updated Apr 11 2023](https://sidearmsports.com/sports/2022/12/7/terms-of-service.aspx) §§2.1–2.3 define that scope; automated recurring app access is not a separately documented API grant. USC/Harvard footer links checked. Terms at other schools may differ. | Bodies checked for 2024/2025, 2024-25/2025-26, selected 2025/2026 baseball/softball. USC football selector reaches 1997, wbb 2010-11, volleyball 2015; Minnesota hockey selector reaches 2018-19. **Selectors are not verified old attendance coverage.** | [USC football 2025](https://usctrojans.com/sports/football/stats/2025/missouri-state/boxscore/34544); [UIndy football 2025](https://athletics.uindy.edu/sports/football/stats/2025/mckendree/boxscore/13573); [Saint Mary’s men’s soccer 2025](https://smcgaels.com/sports/mens-soccer/stats/2025/santa-clara/boxscore/12240) |
| PrestoSports / PrestoStats / PrestoWeb | Attendance is a supported scoring field (basketball Setup); publication is client-dependent. No two or three different-school live box bodies successfully verified here. Do not replace this gap with an unconditional yes. | Classic candidate `/sports/{sportcode}/{season}/boxscores/{YYYYMMDD}_{token}.xml`. **Do not infer XML content from suffix**; body/content type unverified here. [PrestoSync documentation](https://help.prestosports.com/PrestoStats/v1/prestosync-setup-and-download) describes StatCrew XML transfer for authorized operators. [Stats Tab](https://help.prestosports.com/PrestoWeb/v1/stats-tab) describes admin downloads; not a documented anonymous public API. Public-page keys not demonstrated; operator tools require access. | Unresolved. [Presto terms attempted](https://www.prestosports.com/terms-of-service/) returned 403. No permission for nightly app copying established. | 2025-26 candidate routes found; old attendance bodies not verified. NAIA’s Presto submission transition is after 2020-21, not a guarantee of attendance or public archive depth. | [3C2A football 2025-26 candidate](https://www.cccaastats.org/sports/fball/2025-26/boxscores/20250830_2iqk.xml); [NWAC basketball 2025-26 candidate](https://nwacsports.com/sports/mbkb/2025-26/boxscores/20251212_91lb.xml); [basketball Setup cheat sheet](https://help.prestosports.com/__attachments/2621997057/202301_PS_CheatSheets_Basketball.pdf?inst-v=1389d701-24b5-499c-adf1-181f6cfa75e5) (documentation, not game evidence) |
| WMT Digital / Astra | Official school recaps/PDFs can publish game crowds. Native box-score field/API not established for current UCLA/Stanford implementation in this check. A school’s old SIDEARM PDF is not a WMT endpoint. | No verified documented public attendance JSON/XML API or anonymous export. Current UCLA `/sports/football/stats/2025` redirects to a season route; research tool saw shell content. Stanford recap links a box/PDF, but no universal route was tested. Public recaps need no key; an internal API key/access agreement is unknown. | Prior written consent required for scraping/application interaction under [WMT terms §5](https://wmt.digital/terms). No automated endpoint probing after this restriction was identified. | UCLA June 2026 migration means historical statistics must be rechecked. Stanford migration documented July 3 2024. No invented API pattern. | School-specific; not established. | Not established. | Gap: vendor cannot be determined from visual appearance alone. |

**Permission finding is narrower than a legal conclusion:** free browser access and absence of a key are technical observations. Neither establishes permission for a nightly app read. Personal downloading/copying is expressly permitted by SIDEARM; no platform-wide open API/redistribution license was verified. Do not treat this report as clearance to scrape. The blocked Presto and NCAA pages remain unverified rather than targets for bypassing.

### Expanded SIDEARM format and access verification

The checked USC baseball and Long Beach State softball box pages contain a public `__NUXT_DATA__` JSON payload in the HTML response. Its flattened reference array contains a `venue` object with `attendance`, `date`, `start`, `stadium`, `location`, `gameid`, `isNeutral` and home/visitor identifiers; the enclosing box object also supplies `thisTeamIsHomeTeam`. This is **verified embedded JSON**, not a documented standalone attendance API. The URL is the ordinary published box-score URL, no key is required for these reads, and the exact examples are [USC–Pepperdine, 2026-02-13](https://usctrojans.com/sports/baseball/stats/2026/pepperdine/boxscore/35050) and [LBSU–CSUN, 2026-05-02](https://longbeachstate.com/sports/softball/stats/2026/csun/boxscore/10304), read October 26–27. Public JSON presence does not establish permission beyond applicable terms, stability across schools/sports, or a supported API contract. Most importantly, some payloads contain `0` while the visible page omits Attendance: the hidden value must not be promoted to an announced zero. The research fixtures preserve visible publication and raw payload observations separately.

Published access rules checked: [USC robots.txt](https://usctrojans.com/robots.txt), [Boston College robots.txt](https://bceagles.com/robots.txt), [Long Beach State robots.txt](https://longbeachstate.com/robots.txt), and [SIDEARM terms, updated April 11, 2023](https://sidearmsports.com/sports/2022/12/7/terms-of-service.aspx), read October 26, 2026. Cached USC, Boston College, LBSU and Minnesota footers were checked October 27 and link [the current terms entry](https://sidearmsports.com/terms-of-service), which redirects to the same April 11, 2023 terms. **S1:** §§2.1–2.3 permit personal noncommercial downloading/copying with notices retained; copying/storage beyond personal use requires permission. Whether a future personal nightly reader fits that scope is **inference**, not a documented API grant. Original working copies retain notices.

**Archive depth is school-specific.** An older SIDEARM-style official body was also checked: [Whittier at Cal Lutheran, October 8, 2011](https://wcpoets.com/sports/football/stats/2011/cal-lutheran/boxscore/757), read October 27, 2026, publishes **announced** attendance 2,025, Bill Rolland Stadium and kickoff 1:00 pm. This proves that particular historical record remains accessible; it does not prove a continuous 2011–2026 archive or current sponsorship of that program. All worked two-season cohorts use the more recent seasons identified in section 5.

The NCAA’s [Statistics Policies and Guidelines, Attendance section, physical PDF page 13](https://s3.amazonaws.com/fs.ncaa.org/Docs/stats/ForSIDs/Policies.pdf#page=13), current document read 2026-10-26, allows schools’ official figures to be based on turnstile counts, tickets sold or school estimates; championship figures use tickets sold. Thus an official “Attendance” number alone does not identify the method. The policy encourages recording attendance but does not guarantee every box is populated. This report labels an official published number **announced**, even when the institution’s underlying measurement method is unstated.

## 4. Sport-by-sport reach and threshold relevance

A defensible national “typical announced range at large programs” was **not established** from this bounded sample. The table instead gives explicitly bounded observations. Every range below is **estimated observed home range = minimum and maximum of the announced home rows in section 5**, including published zero where present; it is not a predicted crowd, a venue-specific median or a national typical range. Season averages from NCAA reports are labeled aggregate context only. A maximum above the floor proves an observed event, not that the program’s median exceeds it.

## 5. Worked examples — one row per published completed result

**Inventory:** 1,114 distinct program-game rows, including 11 source-marked exhibition/preseason rows. Counts are **estimated counts = number of listed result records**, not estimated attendance. A companion JSON preserves the same factual rows, original game IDs, raw source observations and quality notes. It is a research fixture, not application code.

**Publication rules:** attendance is the announced number from the linked official result/box; blanks stay blank. Published zeros stay raw zeros with their meaning unresolved. When a visible box suppresses a raw embedded zero, that hidden value is not counted as a published zero. If a season table supplies a number omitted from the box, the attendance link points to the season table and the box is linked for metadata. Opponent/publisher identity does not determine the home team. Actual venue is filled only from a checked game record or its completed-game schedule, never from the usual campus room. Times marked scheduled retain the source’s stated time/timezone and are not asserted to be actual first pitch/kickoff. Class follows the completed schedule’s published regular/postseason/exhibition classification; the raw schedule type and named event are preserved in JSON. Explicit postseason event titles take precedence where a generic R flag disagrees. A midseason exhibition remains separate from regular statistics.

**Limits:** 56 blank attendance cells, 370 blank actual venues, 0 unverified classes. Blank venue/class is an unfinished verification field, not a finding that the individual school publishes no such detail. Dates/attendance/status conflicts are itemized in section 6. No complete per-venue median is asserted from these rows.

## 6. Not published, unresolved coverage and reporting quirks

| Finding / distinction | Where checked (season; all read 2026-10-26) | Consequence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Attendance absent in a particular box | BC vs Eastern Kentucky softball, 2026-02-06; [official box](https://bceagles.com/sports/softball/stats/2026/eastern-kentucky-university/boxscore/26607) | Keep blank; do not turn absence into zero or declare all BC softball unpublished. |
| Cume publication does not guarantee a game attendance table | USC baseball 2025/2026, Harvard baseball/softball 2025/2026 and LMU softball 2025/2026 downloaded cume PDFs; exact links in appendix below. | These checked PDFs contain season/player statistics and attendance aggregates, not the required per-game crowd series. Individual boxes may still have attendance. |
| A season PDF can omit a completed game | Harvard football 2025 cume has 10 results; [official season HTML](https://gocrimson.com/sports/football/stats/2025) additionally lists Dartmouth, Nov 1, announced 11,334. | Reconcile final schedule/results count. This report adds only the actually published missing row, not a derived crowd. |
| Another season document can stop before the final game | BC 2026 opening notes, 2025 results p.16, contains 56 completed results through May 21; [2025 official index](https://bceagles.com/sports/baseball/stats/2025) lists 57 box links. | Incomplete even though labeled “All games”; the expanded audit supplies the final official box separately. |
| Published zero is ambiguous | USC and Harvard 2024/2025 results tables have literal zero attendance entries, including neutral games; see section 5. | Retain raw zero separately from missing. Its cause is unstated; no evidence here establishes empty stands, a reporting default, or an automatic exclusion rule. |
| Doubleheaders/sessions can share a crowd | LBSU softball CSUN 2026-05-02 boxes both announce 524; BC 2025 New Orleans doubleheader both 315; NCAA policy p.13. | NCAA policy uses a single date/session figure for baseball/softball doubleheaders (higher figure when two are recorded). Keep games distinct while recognizing they can represent the same attendance session; do not sum as unique people. |
| Hockey session figure is not necessarily one game’s exclusive crowd | NCAA hockey record book: 2025 Beanpot Feb 10 entries share 18,258 at TD Garden; [hockey record book](https://s3.amazonaws.com/fs.ncaa.org/Docs/stats/m_icehockey_rb/Attendance.pdf). Harvard source also includes neutral tournament rows. | Do not mix Beanpot neutral-site sessions with Harvard campus-home history or interpret shared session totals as independent turnstile counts. |
| Official attendance methodology is variable | NCAA [Statistics Policies, p.13](https://s3.amazonaws.com/fs.ncaa.org/Docs/stats/ForSIDs/Policies.pdf#page=13); school boxes typically give only Attendance. | Turnstile, tickets sold or school estimate can be official. Do not relabel every figure tickets distributed. Use “unstated” unless the actual school discloses method. |
| Rounded figures | RSEQ 2025 Montréal/Laval box announces 5,100; many other figures are exact integers. | A rounded-looking number is still the announced value; do not “correct” or claim a rounding policy without evidence. No systematic rounding audit was completed. |
| Neutral is separate from home and separate from metro | USC women’s basketball South Carolina 2025-11-15 is marked vs/neutral in season results despite local Crypto.com Arena; USC TCU 2025 bowl in San Antonio is neutral. | Neither is an ordinary campus home crowd. Source domain and city cannot establish home status or regular/postseason class. |
| Temporary and alternate rooms change the interpretation | Stanford [Apr 19 2025 official recap](https://gostanford.com/news/2025/04/19/13207-fill-stanford-stadium-to-break-ncaa-softball-record) confirms Stanford Stadium while softball park was renovated; USC soccer [Oct 27 2024 box](https://usctrojans.com/sports/womens-soccer/stats/2024/ucla/boxscore/34099) names Dignity Health Sports Park. | Do not carry temporary-room figures to a new/current softball park or USC campus soccer field. NCAA softball selected-record venue text conflicts with primary Stanford/Cal game evidence; primary game source resolves it to Stanford Stadium. |
| Time can mean duration rather than start | Football cume column Time (e.g. 03:30) and game boxes with both Start Time and duration; soccer PDF/HTML may disagree on duration. | No start time inferred from elapsed game duration. Start cell left blank where only duration is published. |
| PDF wrapping can corrupt counts and opponents | USC 2024/2025 soccer cume tables wrap attendance digits; 6,281 can extract as “628 1”, 2,139 as “213 9”. | Attendance was joined only within the known column and row band, not by concatenating arbitrary nearby numbers. Validate against a rendered page/box; player-table numbers must not bleed into fixture fields. |
| Legacy links and website migrations | LMU 2025/2026 legacy box link failed; UCLA moved from SIDEARM to WMT June 2026. | A vendor-pattern guess or archived link is not proof of a live source. Preserve original source identity and reverify. |
| US vendor finding is not Canadian coverage | RSEQ 2025 public HTML and UBC 2025 opponent box were actually checked; Toronto 2024 only indexed clue. | No verified Canadian central attendance API; RSEQ collegiate women’s basketball is now directly verified, while other Canadian basketball/hockey and CCAA/OCAA remain gaps. |
| ONEFA figures not found in checked statistical material | CD Pro Guía [2024 PDF](https://cdpro-guia.com/mayor_14Grandes-2024.pdf), [2025 PDF](https://cdpro-guia.com/mayor_14Grandes-2025.pdf), [2025 results](https://cdpro-guia.com/may14g25.htm); [IPN programs](https://www.ipn.mx/deportes/disciplinas/conjunto.html). | Not published in these checked documents. No verified free announced box history for Águilas Blancas/Burros Blancos; leave history estimate absent. Not a claim that all Mexican institutions publish none. |
| Approximate media crowds do not repair a missing series | La Jornada, Oct 18 2026, [Pumas CU–Águilas Blancas report](https://www.jornada.com.mx/2026/10/18/deportes/a09n1dep); Reporte Índigo Oct 17 2026 [recap](https://www.reporteindigo.com/deportes/pumas-cu-revive-la-mistica-de-los-condores-y-se-lleva-el-clasico-universitario-20261017-0042.html). | Reported “more than” / “near” 35,000 is threshold context only, not an exact announced attendance row. |
| No evidence that the whole lower level omits attendance | NAIA submission FAQ, NJCAA team index, 3C2A/NWAC candidate boxes; access failures noted in tables. | Submission requirements and a supported Attendance field do not establish actual per-school publication. Saint Xavier’s NAIA per-game example and Monroe’s raw-zero NJCAA box are now verified; complete levelwide series and Presto-specific public access remain unresolved. |

### Findings from the expanded game checks

| Issue | Verified evidence and source | Handling implication |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Hidden zero versus visible absence | Some checked USC/BC/LBSU boxes carry raw `venue.attendance = 0` in embedded JSON while omitting visible Attendance. See per-row notes and the source URLs; read October 26–27. | Keep the visible field missing and raw payload value separate. Do not promote the hidden value to announced zero. A checked season result may independently publish a number. |
| Related content contaminates a season link inventory | LBSU’s 2025 statistics page includes links to five 2026 games in its carousel. Legacy and canonical URLs can identify the same game. | Use the results table, source-issued game ID and actual game record; do not count all page links as season games or count URL aliases twice. |
| Doubleheaders are separate games | Distinct box IDs on the same date/opponent can have different figures. The expanded audit keeps the IDs distinct rather than collapsing on date/opponent. | Date + opponent is insufficient as a game identity. A repeated crowd can still represent one admission session. |
| Home/visitor designation is not necessarily the event classification | Some LBSU tournament boxes designate LBSU home while its completed schedule explicitly says neutral; USC’s Corvallis regional also has different away/neutral labels. Exact rows and sources appear below. | Use the explicit schedule neutral classification in the fixture, retaining both source values. A team designated to bat last must not become campus-home history. Other unresolved status disagreements are excluded from derived home ranges. |
| Generic schedule classification flags can disagree with event titles | Some completed school schedule rows have raw type R despite explicit NCAA Tournament, Big Ten Tournament, Big West Championship or Alamo Bowl titles; exact schedule routes appear in fixture metadata. | The fixture uses the explicit named postseason event and retains the raw flag. A generic R is not sufficient to classify every game as regular. |
| A completed exhibition may be outside the statistical cume | Minnesota schedules have completed exhibition results absent from cume records; USC men’s basketball schedules also publish completed exhibitions. | Preserve these separate rows and their published class; do not mix into regular-season medians or invent missing attendance. |
| Canadian aggregate can divide by unmeasured games | RSEQ Dawson 2025-26 results leave most home crowds blank. **Announced aggregate** home total 60 and average 10 over six home games; **estimated arithmetic check** 60 ÷ 6 = 10. [Official results](https://www.rseq-stats.ca/collegial/basketball-f-d1/stats/2526/daw.htm), read 2026-10-26. | This aggregate cannot supply the median of published attendance observations; missing games must not become artificial zero rows. |
| Canadian selected-event figures can conflict across publisher types | For the 2025-10-05 Carleton–Ottawa Panda Game, the [Carleton official recap in its public news index](https://goravens.ca/category/go-ravens-news/) states **announced, index checked** 23,030. [Capital Current, Madeleine Gordon, Hannah Polenz and Kyla Silva, published 2025-10-10](https://capitalcurrent.ca/post-panda-2025-attendance-was-high-mischief-low-as-carleton-uottawa-students-party-responsibly/) states **reported** 24,372 and names TD Place in its photo caption. Both read 2026-10-27. | Preserve the discrepancy and publisher labels; neither number is assigned to Carleton’s TAAG Field history. No home/away/neutral classification or exact usable median fixture is inferred for this separate event. |
| IDs belong to the source, not to a universal game namespace | Harvard’s Belfast Notre Dame record uses school box ID 31379; the linked Notre Dame PDF filename is 35249. Both are preserved via source URLs in that fixture; read 2026-10-27. | A bare numeric ID is not globally unique across publishers, programs or seasons. Reconcile actual games and source identity before deduplicating mirrors. |
| A program’s home games can span several actual venues | USC baseball boxes identify Great Park for [George Washington, 2025-02-14](https://usctrojans.com/sports/baseball/stats/2025/george-washington/boxscore/34358), Page Stadium for [UC Santa Barbara, 2025-03-04](https://usctrojans.com/sports/baseball/stats/2025/uc-santa-barbara/boxscore/34370), and Dedeaux Field for [Pepperdine, 2026-02-13](https://usctrojans.com/sports/baseball/stats/2026/pepperdine/boxscore/35050); read 2026-10-26–27. | Separate the published rooms. A two-season program aggregate would mix different home locations; do not transfer the Great Park/Page Stadium observations to Dedeaux Field. Spelling variants are retained as published rather than silently declared identical. |
| Venue-specific hockey history differs from a city aggregate | Denver’s official 2025-26 summary identifies the separate Ball Arena event; its selected 2026-02-07 Colorado College box names Magness Arena and announces 7,082. [Official Denver notes](https://s3.us-east-2.amazonaws.com/sidearm.nextgen.sites/denverpioneers.com/documents/2026/2/12/20260213_DU_HKY_Game_Notes_Omaha_Weekend_Release.pdf), [official preview discussing both rooms](https://denverpioneers.com/news/2026/10/21/-4-ice-hockey-preview-no-4-pioneers-hosting-no-18-boston-college-on-homecoming-weekend), read 2026-10-26. | A city/program season average is not a per-room median. |
| Legacy links can be stale even on a readable season page | Georgetown and Cleveland State 2025-26 season HTML lists legacy `boxscore.aspx` links whose sampled first games returned 404. Independently indexed canonical school boxes then verified Georgetown–Morgan State (4,208, Capital One Arena) and Cleveland State–Capital (1,450, Henry J. Goodman Arena); exact links/read dates appear in keyed coverage. | Record route-specific failure separately from publication. Follow a published canonical route; do not label the program’s attendance Not published or guess an API. |

## 7. Recommendation

For a personal noncommercial reader within the applicable terms, SIDEARM’s most concrete free route in this study is the school’s published completed-game index plus the visible official box-score Attendance field, with a verified season results table/PDF as a lower-request-count source or cross-check when it actually contains per-game crowds; the embedded JSON can assist parsing but hidden zero defaults must not replace missing publication. Respect school-specific robots/access rules and obtain permission for uses beyond the personal-copy scope or other applicable restrictions. Presto needs direct client/body/terms verification or an owner-provided export; operator StatCrew XML tooling is not a public anonymous API. WMT and Disney/ESPN require the relevant permission for automated access under their stated restrictions, so no guessed internal endpoint is recommended. RSEQ’s verified static HTML is a technically useful football and collegiate-basketball source, with reuse terms unresolved; Canada-wide coverage and the sampled Mexican programs’ announced histories remain incomplete. A median would be misleading if neutral, postseason or exhibition results were pooled with ordinary home games, alternate/temporary venues were merged, missing or suppressed figures became zeros, doubleheader/session crowds were treated as independent admissions, or source-date conflicts changed the day class. Use no history-based estimate for a program/venue without verified published history; season averages and media approximations cannot supply missing games.

## Remaining gaps and confidence boundary

The selected two-season source cohorts and full game rows do not amount to a census of all programs in the covered metros. Older archive bodies beyond the checked years, many Harvard game-level venues and some published-time/timezone details, live Presto bodies and client-specific terms, a documented permitted WMT feed, NCAA/ESPN game-field/archive verification, additional Canadian conferences and sports, and an official Mexican university attendance series remain unresolved. Women’s hockey, men’s lacrosse and gymnastics have threshold evidence but no full two-metro/two-season worked set here; the seven primary worked families are football, basketball, men’s ice hockey, baseball, softball, women’s volleyball and women’s soccer. These are recorded research gaps, not guesses that the figures never exist. This answer remains proposal material; it neither changes the existing app rules nor authorizes a build.
